带冷却期的加权区间选择\n23: \n24: operation：`weighted_intervals`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n25: \n26: 输入首对象{windows:[{id:非空str,start:int>=0,end:int>start,value:int}]}，契约cooldown:int>=0。id去首尾空白后唯一；重复id即ValueError。半开区间相容条件为较早窗口end+cooldown<=较晚窗口start。输出result={selected:[排序后的规范化id],value:所选value之和}。优化顺序：总value最大；再比较排序后的整个ID列表，按通常字典序取最小，较短的相同前缀列表更小。允许负value和0值，允许空集合；若最优价值为0，空列表优于所有非空同价值列表。不得只给DP局部状态按单个ID打破平局，也不能仅因窗口value为0就一概排除它——它可能改变正价值最优解的完整ID列表平局。空windows为selected=[]、value=0。本operation只消费首输入，契约threshold不参与目标。\n27: \n28: 局部检查命令：`python3 engines/rebalance_windows/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n29: \n30: ### engines/resource_order — 依赖图与确定性关键路径\n31: \n32: operation：`critical_path`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n33: \n34: 首输入{nodes:[{id:非空str,duration:int>=0,deps:list[id]}]}。节点id去首尾空白后唯一，节点输出id为去空白值；依赖引用必须准确匹配这些id。重复id/依赖、未知依赖、自依赖和环均ValueError。后续组件result若有feasible字段：false直接返回{blocked:true,waves:[],critical_path:[],makespan:0,earliest_finish:{}}；true则读取assignments中的job/worker，同一worker的job按jobid升序，相邻job添加先后依赖。新增边也必须无环；未知被分配job报错；未分配节点仍按原依赖存在。没有分配结果时绝不凭空添加人员依赖。每波取本波开始时全部就绪节点并按id排序；finish=duration+最大前驱finish。最大finish的前驱并列时，比较前驱已经形成的完整路径（不含当前节点）取字典序最小，然后追加当前节点；最后在最大finish终点间按其完整路径列表取最小。正常result={blocked:false,waves,critical_path,makespan,earliest_finish}，earliest_finish按id；空图的两个列表与映射为空、makespan=0。不要把分配结果封装的name/result两层误当作assignments。\n35: \n36: 局部检查命令：`python3 engines/resource_order/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n37: \n38: ### engines/operator_assignment — 技能容量约束分配\n39: \n40: operation：`capacity_assignment`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n41: \n42: 首输入{jobs:[{id,skill:非空str,duration:int>=1}],workers:[{id,skills:list[非空str],capacity:int>=0,cost:int>=0,affinity:{job_id:int}}]}。分别检查jobs/workers的id为非空文本且去首尾空白后的id不重复；实际标识、技能匹配、输出及排序按原字符串使用，不进行大小写转换。每个job必须分配恰好一次，worker必须有完全相同的skill，且所分配duration之和<=capacity。后续输入逐个解开组件result：存在selected键时，仅保留id位于selected中的worker（selected不是job列表）；存在makespan键时，把每个剩余worker.capacity缩为min(capacity,makespan)，不是总容量。可行解依次最大化sum(worker.affinity.get(job.id,0))，最小化sum(worker.cost*job.duration)，最后按job升序形成(job,worker)元组列表并取字典序最小。result={feasible,score,cost,assignments:[{job,worker}]}，assignments按job排序。无可行分配为false/0/0/[]；没有job时为true/0/0/[]，即使worker也为空。不加入每个worker只能接一个job的限制。\n43: \n44: 局部检查命令：`python3 engines/operator_assignment/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n45: \n46: ## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n47: \n48: 所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\n49: `run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 integrate.py`读取组件产物生成`outputs/core_manifest.json`，其完整结构为`{\"components\": {组件名: 该组件的result}}`。\n50: \n51: `final_report.py`读取并解析完整`outputs/core_manifest.json`对象，写`outputs/final_report.json`，恰有title、component_count、sha256、attention四个字段。title准确为`租户配额再平衡`；component_count为core_object[\"components\"]的键数。\n52: sha256的输入对象是完整core_object，包含外层components键；不是仅core_object[\"components\"]、不是原文件字节、不是多个文件摘要拼接。序列化准确为`json.dumps(core_object, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`，不加文件末尾换行，然后求SHA-256小写hex。\n53: attention是组件名去重排序列表：对每个result，blocked为真、feasible或compatible显式为false、deny>0、score_total>=20中任一条件成立即列入；不存在的字段不触发，不能把空列表、空对象或数值0等同于显式false。每个组件至多列一次。\n54: \n55: ## 验收与执行边界\n56: \n57: 在工作区运行`python3 check.py`。公开检查会清理旧outputs、完成适用的材料物化、运行run_all并检查模块与输出结构；公开通过不代表全部业务语义正确。外部只读验证器另外检查受保护文件、全部预期产物、规范序列化及接口边界输入。\n58: 给Agent的材料只有本任务TASK.md和workspace。作者参考实现、评分器、审计记录、历史输出和其他任务不得作为工作区或模型上下文输入。\n\n(End of 带冷却期的加权区间选择\n\noperation：`weighted_intervals`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入首对象{windows:[{id:非空str,start:int>=0,end:int>start,value:int}]}，契约cooldown:int>=0。id去首尾空白后唯一；重复id即ValueError。半开区间相容条件为较早窗口end+cooldown<=较晚窗口start。输出result={selected:[排序后的规范化id],value:所选value之和}。优化顺序：总value最大；再比较排序后的整个ID列表，按通常字典序取最小，较短的相同前缀列表更小。允许负value和0值，允许空集合；若最优价值为0，空列表优于所有非空同价值列表。不得只给DP局部状态按单个ID打破平局，也不能仅因窗口value为0就一概排除它——它可能改变正价值最优解的完整ID列表平局。空windows为selected=[]、value=0。本operation只消费首输入，契约threshold不参与目标。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 engines/rebalance_windows/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### engines/resource_order — 依赖图与确定性关键路径\n\noperation：`critical_path`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n首输入{nodes:[{id:非空str,duration:int>=0,deps:list[id]}]}。节点id去首尾空白后唯一，节点输出id为去空白值；依赖引用必须准确匹配这些id。重复id/依赖、未知依赖、自依赖和环均ValueError。后续组件result若有feasible字段：false直接返回{blocked:true,waves:[],critical_path:[],makespan:0,earliest_finish:{}}；true则读取assignments中的job/worker，同一worker的job按jobid升序，相邻job添加先后依赖。新增边也必须无环；未知被分配job报错；未分配节点仍按原依赖存在。没有分配结果时绝不凭空添加人员依赖。每波取本波开始时全部就绪节点并按id排序；finish=duration+最大前驱finish。最大finish的前驱并列时，比较前驱已经形成的完整路径（不含当前节点）取字典序最小，然后追加当前节点；最后在最大finish终点间按其完整路径列表取最小。正常result={blocked:false,waves,critical_path,makespan,earliest_finish}，earliest_finish按id；空图的两个列表与映射为空、makespan=0。不要把分配结果封装的name/result两层误当作assignments。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 engines/resource_order/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### engines/operator_assignment — 技能容量约束分配\n\noperation：`capacity_assignment`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n首输入{jobs:[{id,skill:非空str,duration:int>=1}],workers:[{id,skills:list[非空str],capacity:int>=0,cost:int>=0,affinity:{job_id:int}}]}。分别检查jobs/workers的id为非空文本且去首尾空白后的id不重复；实际标识、技能匹配、输出及排序按原字符串使用，不进行大小写转换。每个job必须分配恰好一次，worker必须有完全相同的skill，且所分配duration之和<=capacity。后续输入逐个解开组件result：存在selected键时，仅保留id位于selected中的worker（selected不是job列表）；存在makespan键时，把每个剩余worker.capacity缩为min(capacity,makespan)，不是总容量。可行解依次最大化sum(worker.affinity.get(job.id,0))，最小化sum(worker.cost*job.duration)，最后按job升序形成(job,worker)元组列表并取字典序最小。result={feasible,score,cost,assignments:[{job,worker}]}，assignments按job排序。无可行分配为false/0/0/[]；没有job时为true/0/0/[]，即使worker也为空。不加入每个worker只能接一个job的限制。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 engines/operator_assignment/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n## Compute:\n- waves: each wave = all ready nodes at start of wave, sorted by id. \"每波取本波开始时全部就绪节点并按id排序\".\n- finish = duration + max predecessor finish. (max over all predecessors' finish).\n- makespan = max finish over all nodes (0 if empty graph).\n- critical_path: a list of node ids forming the critical path. Ties broken by lexicographic full path.\n\nLet me understand critical path tie-breaking precisely:\n\n\"finish=duration+最大前驱finish。最大finish的前驱并列时，比较前驱已经形成的完整路径（不含当前节点）取字典序最小，然后追加当前节点；最后在最大finish终点间按其完整路径列表取最小。\"\n\nSo each node has a \"complete path\" = the critical path leading to that node (a list of node ids from start to that node). For a node, its predecessors each have their own complete path (ending at that predecessor). If multiple predecessors achieve the max finish, choose the predecessor whose complete path (the path ending at that predecessor, not including current node) is lexicographically smallest. Then append current node to that path → node's complete path.\n\nFor the final critical_path: among nodes with max finish (makespan), choose the one whose complete path is lexicographically smallest.\n\nLet me formalize. For each node, compute:\n- finish[v] = duration[v] + max(0, max_{p in preds} finish[p]).\n- path[v] = list of ids from a start node to v, representing the chosen critical path to v.\n\nFor a source node (no predecessors): finish = duration; path = [id_v].\n\nFor a node v with predecessors: let maxfin = max finish[p]. Consider predecessors p with finish[p] == maxfin. Among these, choose p* with lexicographically smallest path[p]. Then path[v] = path[p*] + [id_v]. If no predecessors, path[v] = [id_v].\n\nFinally, among all nodes with finish == makespan, choose the one with lexicographically smallest path[v]; critical_path = path[v*]. If graph empty, critical_path = [].\n\nWait, \"最后在最大finish终点间按其完整路径列表取最小\" — among the max-finish end nodes, take the one with minimal complete path list. Yes.\n\nNow let me carefully implement each component.\n\nLet me write the rebalance_windows transform.\n\n### rebalance_windows transform.py\n\n```\nimport json, math\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\ndef _is_int(x):\n    return isinstance(x, int) and not isinstance(x, bool)\n\ndef _finite_num(x):\n    return isinstance(x, (int,float)) and not isinstance(x, bool) and math.isfinite(x)\n\ndef transform(payloads, contract):\n    if len(payloads) < 1:\n        raise ValueError(\"rebalance_windows requires 1 input\")\n    raw = payloads[0]\n    data = _unwrap(raw)\n    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n        raise ValueError(\"input must be object\")\n    if \"windows\" not in data:\n        raise ValueError(\"missing windows\")\n    windows_raw = data[\"windows\"]\n    if not isinstance(windows_raw, list):\n        raise ValueError(\"windows must be list\")\n    cooldown = contract.get(\"cooldown\")\n    if not _is_int(cooldown) or cooldown < 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"cooldown must be int >=0\")\n    intervals = []\n    seen = set()\n    for w in windows_raw:\n        if not isinstance(w, dict):\n            raise ValueError(\"window must be object\")\n        wid = w.get(\"id\")\n        if not isinstance(wid, str) or wid.strip() == \"\":\n            raise ValueError(\"window id must be non-empty str\")\n        wid_s = wid.strip()\n        if wid_s in seen:\n            raise ValueError(\"duplicate window id\")\n        seen.add(wid_s)\n        start = w.get(\"start\"); end = w.get(\"end\"); value = w.get(\"value\")\n        if not _is_int(start) or start < 0: raise ValueError(...)\n        if not _is_int(end) or end <= start: raise ValueError(...)\n        if not _is_int(value): raise ValueError(...)\n        intervals.append({\"id\": wid_s, \"start\": start, \"end\": end, \"value\": value})\n    # algorithm\n    ...\n    selected, total = solve(intervals, cooldown)\n    return {\"name\": contract[\"name\"], \"result\": {\"selected\": selected, \"value\": total}}\n```\n\nWait, \"end:int>start\" — end must be int > start. So check _is_int(end) and end > start. Also start >= 0. Good.\n\nvalue int (no constraint, can be negative/zero). So just _is_int(value).\n\nNow the solve function.\n\nLet me implement max_weight and greedy.\n\n```\ndef _compatible(a, b, c):\n    # a, b intervals dicts; a.start <= b.start assumed? We'll compute based on start.\n    if a[\"start\"] <= b[\"start\"]:\n        return a[\"end\"] + c <= b[\"start\"]\n    else:\n        return b[\"end\"] + c <= a[\"start\"]\n\ndef _compatible_with_all(j, chosen_sorted_by_start, c):\n    # chosen_sorted_by_start: list sorted by start\n    for x in chosen_sorted_by_start:\n        if not _compatible(x, j, c):  # works regardless of order? _compatible handles order by start\n            return False\n    return True\n```\n\nActually `_compatible(x, j, c)` handles order internally. Good. But O(k) per call.\n\nmax_weight on a list of intervals (subset):\n```\ndef _max_weight(intervals, c):\n    sub = sorted(intervals, key=lambda x: (x[\"end\"], x[\"start\"], x[\"id\"]))\n    n = len(sub)\n    if n == 0: return 0\n    dp = [0]*n\n    for i in range(n):\n        best = dp[i-1] if i>0 else 0\n        # find predecessor\n        j = i-1\n        while j >= 0 and sub[j][\"end\"] + c > sub[i][\"start\"]:\n            j -= 1\n        take = sub[i][\"value\"] + (dp[j] if j>=0 else 0)\n        if take > best:\n            best = take\n        dp[i] = best\n    return dp[-1]\n```\n\nWait, but the recurrence f(i) = max(f(i-1), f(p(i)) + w_i) where p(i) is the largest index j with end_j + c <= start_i. Using while loop from i-1 down to find largest j satisfying end_j + c <= start_i. Since sorted by end ascending, end_j increasing with j, so end_j + c <= start_i holds for j <= some threshold. The largest such j is found by scanning down. Correct. dp[j] for j = largest predecessor. Good. And since dp is non-decreasing? Not necessarily monotonic but dp[j] is the max weight in first j+1. Standard.\n\nActually the standard recurrence uses dp[i] = max(dp[i-1], weight_i + dp[p(i)]), where p(i) is largest index with finish <= start_i. My while loop finds that p(i). Good. But I must ensure dp array indexed correctly: dp[i] = max weight using sub[0..i]. dp[p(i)] for p(i) < i. Good.\n\nFor empty subset, return 0 (empty set weight 0). Good.\n\nGreedy solve:\n```\ndef solve(intervals, c):\n    n = len(intervals)\n    if n == 0:\n        return [], 0\n    W = _max_weight(intervals, c)\n    # sort ids\n    by_id = sorted(intervals, key=lambda x: x[\"id\"])\n    id_to_int = {x[\"id\"]: x for x in intervals}\n    chosen = []  # list of interval dicts (chosen), sorted by id as we add\n    pos = 0\n    while True:\n        if sum(x[\"value\"] for x in chosen) == W:\n            break\n        found = None\n        for i in range(pos, n):\n            x = by_id[i]\n            # check compatibility with chosen\n            if not _compatible_with_all(x, chosen_sorted, c):\n                continue\n            # can_extend\n            cand = chosen + [x]\n            if _can_extend(cand, intervals, c, W):\n                found = i\n                break\n        if found is None:\n            # should not happen\n            break\n        chosen.append(by_id[found])\n        pos = found + 1\n        # keep chosen sorted by id? chosen is in increasing id order already (by_id sorted). Good.\n    selected = sorted(x[\"id\"] for x in chosen)\n    total = sum(x[\"value\"] for x in chosen)\n    return selected, total\n```\n\nWait, `chosen_sorted` for compatibility check: I need chosen sorted by start for _compatible_with_all (which iterates and uses _compatible that handles order, so order doesn't matter actually). Raw local objects are not file paths or lists.\n- Type rules:\n  - int fields strictly exclude `bool`.\n  - Finite numbers exclude bool, NaN, Infinity.\n  - Non-empty text: strip then require non-empty.\n  - Lists/dicts read by type; do not treat `null` as empty or coerce number strings.\n- Missing required fields, invalid types, explicit conflicts -> `ValueError`.\n- Lexicographic order: normal string/list/tuple order; shorter same-prefix list is smaller.\n\n### `engines/rebalance_windows` weighted intervals rules\n- First input object: `{\"windows\":[{\"id\": nonempty str, \"start\": int>=0, \"end\": int>start, \"value\": int}]}`.\n- `cooldown` from contract, int >= 0.\n- Window ids trimmed for uniqueness and output; duplicate trimmed id -> `ValueError`.\n- Compatibility: earlier window `end + cooldown <= later window start`.\n- Output `result = {\"selected\": [sorted trimmed ids], \"value\": sum}`.\n- Optimization order:\n  1. Maximize total value.\n  2. Among optimal value sets, lexicographically smallest sorted ID list.\n- Allow negative and zero values, allow empty set. If optimal value is 0, empty list beats all nonempty same-value lists.\n- Zero-value windows may affect lex tie-break and must not be ignored.\n- Simple per-ID DP local tie-break is incorrect.\n- Important derived correct algorithm:\n  - Compute `W = max_weight(all intervals)`.\n  - Sorted-by-id greedy prefix construction:\n    - While `value(chosen) != W`: scan ids ascending, test candidate `x` where `x` is compatible with all chosen and `can_extend(chosen ∪ {x})`.\n    - `can_extend(C)` = `weight(C) + max_weight(allowed \\ C) == W`, where allowed = all intervals compatible with every interval in `C`.\n    - Stop when `value(chosen) == W`.\n  - This avoids the known false DP combine where lex-min prefix is insufficient.\n\n### `engines/resource_order` critical path rules\n- First input object: `{\"nodes\":[{\"id\": nonempty str, \"duration\": int>=0, \"deps\": list of ids}]}`.\n- Node trimmed ids unique; output node id is trimmed value.\n- `deps` must exactly match trimmed node ids.\n- Duplicate node id, duplicate deps within a node, unknown dep, self dep, cycle -> `ValueError`.\n- Normal output: `{blocked: false, waves, critical_path, makespan, earliest_finish}`.\n- Blocked output: `{blocked: true, waves: [], critical_path: [], makespan: 0, earliest_finish: {}}`.\n- Waves: each wave contains all ready nodes at start of that wave, sorted by id.\n- `finish = duration + max(predecessor finish)`.\n- Critical path tie-break:\n  - For each node, among max-finish predecessors choose predecessor with lexicographically smallest complete path ending at that predecessor, then append current node.\n  - Final `critical_path`: among nodes with max `finish`, choose lexicographically smallest complete path.\n- `earliest_finish`: dict id -> finish, sorted/keys by id.\n- Subsequent inputs may carry component results with `feasible`:\n  - `feasible: false` -> immediately return blocked result.\n  - `feasible: true` -> read `assignments` list `{job, worker}`, group jobs by worker, sort job ids ascending, add dependencies between consecutive jobs.\n  - Unknown assigned job -> error; added edges must remain acyclic; unassigned nodes keep original deps.\n  - Actual given contract has only one input, so no assignment is consumed here in the real pipeline.\n\n### `engines/operator_assignment` capacity assignment rules\n- First input object: `{\"jobs\": [...], \"workers\": [...]}`.\n- Jobs:\n  - `id` nonempty str, trimmed uniqueness only; original string used for output/matching/sorting.\n  - `skill` nonempty str.\n  - `duration` int >= 1.\n- Workers:\n  - `id` nonempty str, trimmed uniqueness only; original string used.\n  - `skills` list of nonempty strings.\n  - `capacity` int >= 0.\n  - `cost` int >= 0.\n  - `affinity` dict `{job_id: int}`.\n- Subsequent inputs processed in order:\n  - If result has `selected`: keep only workers whose original id is in `selected`.\n  - If result has `makespan`: for each remaining worker set `capacity = min(capacity, makespan)`.\n- Assignment constraints:\n  - Every job assigned exactly once.\n  - Worker must have exactly the job skill in `worker.skills`.\n  - Sum durations assigned to worker <= worker capacity.\n  - No “one worker can take only one job” limit.\n- Objective lex order:\n  1. Maximize total affinity: `sum(worker.affinity.get(job.id, 0))`.\n  2. Minimize total cost: `sum(worker.cost * job.duration)`.\n  3. Among ties, sort assignments by job id ascending and take lexicographically smallest `(job, worker)` tuple list.\n- Output `result`: `{feasible, score, cost, assignments}`.\n  - `score` = maximized affinity sum.\n  - `cost` = minimized cost sum.\n  - `assignments` list of `{job, worker}` sorted by job.\n  - No feasible assignment: `{feasible: false, score: 0, cost: 0, assignments: []}`.\n  - No jobs: `{feasible: true, score: 0, cost: 0, assignments: []}` even if workers empty.\n\n### Root orchestration and final report\n- `run_all.py`:\n  - Discover all `contract.json` excluding `outputs/`.\n  - Build dependency order from actual input/output paths, not filename sort.\n  - For each contract in topo order run its `render.py` as subprocess with cwd `/app`.\n  - Then run `/app/integrate.py` then `/app/final_report.py` as subprocesses.\n  - Propagate non-zero exit codes.\n- `final_report.py`:\n  - Read parsed full `outputs/core_manifest.json`.\n  - `title = \"租户配额再平衡\"`.\n  - `component_count = len(core_object[\"components\"])`.\n  - `sha256` = lowercase hex SHA-256 of:\n    - `json.dumps(core_object, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`.\n  - `attention`: sorted unique component names where any of:\n    - `result.get(\"blocked\") is True`\n    - `result.get(\"feasible\") is False`\n    - `result.get(\"compatible\") is False`\n    - `result.get(\"deny\", 0) > 0`\n    - `result.get(\"score_total\", 0) >= 20`\n  - Missing keys do not trigger; explicit false only; empty/zero/null not same as false.\n- Existing `integrate.py` writes `outputs/core_manifest.json` as `{\"components\": {sorted component name: result}}`.\n\n### Known sample-derived expectations\n- `resource_order` from current input was reasoned as:\n  - `waves = [[\"j0\",\"j1\"], [\"j2\",\"j3\"], [\"j4\",\"j5\"]]`\n  - `makespan = 10`\n  - `critical_path = [\"j1\",\"j3\",\"j5\"]`\n  - `earliest_finish = {\"j0\":2,\"j1\":3,\"j2\":6,\"j3\":8,\"j4\":7,\"j5\":10}`\n- `operator_assignment` feasible/score/cost still to be computed after implementation.\n\n## Work State\n### Completed\n- Explored `/app` structure, read contracts, inputs, check/integrate flow, and check modules.\n\n### Active\n- No files implemented yet. JSON，对象或数组顶层合法；非法JSON、顶层null/布尔/数字/字符串均抛ValueError。业务字段由transform检查。\n13: - `transform.transform(payloads: list, contract: dict)`：payloads严格按该契约inputs的顺序传入已读取的对象或数组，返回`{\"name\": contract[\"name\"], contract中的name/operation/inputs/output是接口字段；只有下列业务规则显式使用的算法参数参与计算，不能看到threshold便对所有operation筛选。参数值读取本组件契约，不从样例推断固定常量。\n19: \n20: ## 各组件的完整业务规则\n21: \n22: ### engines/rebalance_windows — 带冷却期的加权区间选择\n23: \n24: operation：`weighted_intervals`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n25: \n26: 输入首对象{windows:[{id:非空str,start:int>=0,end:int>start,value:int}]}，契约cooldown:int>=0。id去首尾空白后唯一；重复id即ValueError。半开区间相容条件为较早窗口end+cooldown<=较晚窗口start。输出result={selected:[排序后的规范化id],value:所选value之和}。优化顺序：总value最大；再比较排序后的整个ID列表，按通常字典序取最小，较短的相同前缀列表更小。允许负value和0值，允许空集合；若最优价值为0，空列表优于所有非空同价值列表。不得只给DP局部状态按单个ID打破平局，也不能仅因窗口value为0就一概排除它——它可能改变正价值最优解的完整ID列表平局。空windows为selected=[]、value=0。本operation只消费首输入，契约threshold不参与目标。\n27: \n28: 局部检查命令：`python3 engines/rebalance_windows/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n29: \n30: ### engines/resource_order — 依赖图与确定性关键路径\n31: \n32: operation：`critical_path`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n33: \n34: 首输入{nodes:[{id:非空str,duration:int>=0,deps:list[id]}]}。节点id去首尾空白后唯一，节点输出id为去空白值；依赖引用必须准确匹配这些id。重复id/依赖、未知依赖、自依赖和环均ValueError。后续组件result若有feasible字段：false直接返回{blocked:true,waves:[],critical_path:[],makespan:0,earliest_finish:{}}；true则读取assignments中的job/worker，同一worker的job按jobid升序，相邻job添加先后依赖。新增边也必须无环；未知被分配job报错；未分配节点仍按原依赖存在。没有分配结果时绝不凭空添加人员依赖。每波取本波开始时全部就绪节点并按id排序；finish=duration+最大前驱finish。最大finish的前驱并列时，比较前驱已经形成的完整路径（不含当前节点）取字典序最小，然后追加当前节点；最后在最大finish终点间按其完整路径列表取最小。正常result={blocked:false,waves,critical_path,makespan,earliest_finish}，earliest_finish按id；空图的两个列表与映射为空、makespan=0。不要把分配结果封装的name/result两层误当作assignments。\n35: operation：`capacity_assignment`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n41: \n42: 首输入{jobs:[{id,skill:非空str,duration:int>=1}],workers:[{id,skills:list[非空str],capacity:int>=0,cost:int>=0,affinity:{job_id:int}}]}。分别检查jobs/workers的id为非空文本且去首尾空白后的id不重复；实际标识、技能匹配、输出及排序按原字符串使用，不进行大小写转换。每个job必须分配恰好一次，worker必须有完全相同的skill，且所分配duration之和<=capacity。后续输入逐个解开组件result：存在selected键时，仅保留id位于selected中的worker（selected不是job列表）；存在makespan键时，把每个剩余worker.capacity缩为min(capacity,makespan)，不是总容量。可行解依次最大化sum(worker.affinity.get(job.id,0))，最小化sum(worker.cost*job.duration)，最后按job升序形成(job,worker)元组列表并取字典序最小。result={feasible,score,cost,assignments:[{job,worker}]}，assignments按job排序。无可行分配为false/0/0/[]；没有job时为true/0/0/[]，即使worker也为空。不加入每个worker只能接一个job的限制。\n43: \n44: 局部检查命令：`python3 engines/operator_assignment/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n45: \n46: ## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n47: \n48: 所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\n49: `run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 integrate.py`读取组件产物生成`outputs/core_manifest.json`，其完整结构为`{\"components\": {组件名: 该组件的result}}`。\n50: \n51: `final_report.py`读取并解析完整`outputs/core_manifest.json`对象，写`outputs/final_report.json`，恰有title、component_count、sha256、attention四个字段。title准确为`租户配额再平衡`；component_count为core_object[\"components\"]的键数。\n52: sha256的输入对象是完整core_object，包含外层components键；不是仅core_object[\"components\"]、不是原文件字节、不是多个文件摘要拼接。序列化准确为`json.dumps(core_object, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`，不加文件末尾换行，然后求SHA-256小写hex。\n53: 带冷却期的加权区间选择\n\noperation：`weighted_intervals`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入首对象{windows:[{id:非空str,start:int>=0,end:int>start,value:int}]}，契约cooldown:int>=0。id去首尾空白后唯一；重复id即ValueError。半开区间相容条件为较早窗口end+cooldown<=较晚窗口start。输出result={selected:[排序后的规范化id],value:所选value之和}。优化顺序：总value最大；再比较排序后的整个ID列表，按通常字典序取最小，较短的相同前缀列表更小。允许负value和0值，允许空集合；若最优价值为0，空列表优于所有非空同价值列表。不得只给DP局部状态按单个ID打破平局，也不能仅因窗口value为0就一概排除它——它可能改变正价值最优解的完整ID列表平局。空windows为selected=[]、value=0。本operation只消费首输入，契约threshold不参与目标。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 engines/rebalance_windows/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### engines/resource_order — 依赖图与确定性关键路径\n\noperation：`critical_path`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n首输入{nodes:[{id:非空str,duration:int>=0,deps:list[id]}]}。节点id去首尾空白后唯一，节点输出id为去空白值；依赖引用必须准确匹配这些id。重复id/依赖、未知依赖、自依赖和环均ValueError。后续组件result若有feasible字段：false直接返回{blocked:true,waves:[],critical_path:[],makespan:0,earliest_finish:{}}；true则读取assignments中的job/worker，同一worker的job按jobid升序，相邻job添加先后依赖。新增边也必须无环；未知被分配job报错；未分配节点仍按原依赖存在。没有分配结果时绝不凭空添加人员依赖。每波取本波开始时全部就绪节点并按id排序；finish=duration+最大前驱finish。最大finish的前驱并列时，比较前驱已经形成的完整路径（不含当前节点）取字典序最小，然后追加当前节点；最后在最大finish终点间按其完整路径列表取最小。正常result={blocked:false,waves,critical_path,makespan,earliest_finish}，earliest_finish按id；空图的两个列表与映射为空、makespan=0。不要把分配结果封装的name/result两层误当作assignments。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 engines/resource_order/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### engines/operator_assignment — 技能容量约束分配\n\noperation：`capacity_assignment`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n首输入{jobs:[{id,skill:非空str,duration:int>=1}],workers:[{id,skills:list[非空str],capacity:int>=0,cost:int>=0,affinity:{job_id:int}}]}。分别检查jobs/workers的id为非空文本且去首尾空白后的id不重复；实际标识、技能匹配、输出及排序按原字符串使用，不进行大小写转换。每个job必须分配恰好一次，worker必须有完全相同的skill，且所分配duration之和<=capacity。后续输入逐个解开组件result：存在selected键时，仅保留id位于selected中的worker（selected不是job列表）；存在makespan键时，把每个剩余worker.capacity缩为min(capacity,makespan)，不是总容量。可行解依次最大化sum(worker.affinity.get(job.id,0))，最小化sum(worker.cost*job.duration)，最后按job升序形成(job,worker)元组列表并取字典序最小。result={feasible,score,cost,assignments:[{job,worker}]}，assignments按job排序。无可行分配为false/0/0/[]；没有job时为true/0/0/[]，即使worker也为空。不加入每个worker只能接一个job的限制。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 engines/operator_assignment/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n## 公共函数接口与输入解释\n\n- `model.load_payload(path: Path)`：读取UTF-8 JSON，对象或数组顶层合法；非法JSON、顶层null/布尔/数字/字符串均抛ValueError。业务字段由transform检查。\n- `transform.transform(payloads: list, contract: dict)`：payloads严格按该契约inputs的顺序传入已读取的对象或数组，返回`{\"name\": contract[\"name\"], \"result\": 算法结果对象}`。输出恰有name/result两层；本说明各operation的输出字段均属于result，不得额外嵌套一次name/result。不得修改payloads或contract。\n- 输入对象仅当键集合恰好是`{\"name\",\"result\"}`时视为上游组件封装；先读取其result，再使用对应operation约定的字段。原始本地对象不是文件路径，也不当成records或nodes列表。多输入operation必须消费全部指定输入，不能复制固定上游结果。\n- `render.build_component(root: Path, contract_path: Path)`：读取该契约及其全部输入，调用transform并返回完整封装；执行组件render.py时写入contract.output。文件路径相对于工作区root；从任意当前目录启动均应正确，缺文件或子命令失败须失败。\n- int字段严格排除bool；有限数字允许int/float但排除bool、NaN、Infinity。非空文本先检查去首尾空白后不为空；是否返回去空白/小写形式由各operation明确规定，不全局改写身份字段。列表与字典按规定类型读取，不把null当空集合，不自动把数字字符串转换成数字。\n- 文中没有给出默认值的必要字段缺失、非法类型或明确列出的冲突抛ValueError；合法无解使用operation的无解结果，不等同于输入错误。词典序采用通常字符串/列表/元组顺序，相同前缀时较短列表更小。\n- contract中的name/operation/inputs/output是接口字段；只有下列业务规则显式使用的算法参数参与计算，不能看到threshold便对所有operation筛选。参数值读取本组件契约，不从样例推断固定常量。\n\n## 各组件的完整业务规则\n\n### engines/rebalance_windows — 带冷却期的加权区间选择\n\noperation：`weighted_intervals`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n输入首对象{windows:[{id:非空str,start:int>=0,end:int>start,value:int}]}，契约cooldown:int>=0。id去首尾空白后唯一；重复id即ValueError。半开区间相容条件为较早窗口end+cooldown<=较晚窗口start。输出result={selected:[排序后的规范化id],value:所选value之和}。优化顺序：总value最大；再比较排序后的整个ID列表，按通常字典序取最小，较短的相同前缀列表更小。允许负value和0值，允许空集合；若最优价值为0，空列表优于所有非空同价值列表。不得只给DP局部状态按单个ID打破平局，也不能仅因窗口value为0就一概排除它——它可能改变正价值最优解的完整ID列表平局。空windows为selected=[]、value=0。本operation只消费首输入，契约threshold不参与目标。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 engines/rebalance_windows/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### engines/resource_order — 依赖图与确定性关键路径\n\noperation：`critical_path`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n首输入{nodes:[{id:非空str,duration:int>=0,deps:list[id]}]}。节点id去首尾空白后唯一，节点输出id为去空白值；依赖引用必须准确匹配这些id。重复id/依赖、未知依赖、自依赖和环均ValueError。后续组件result若有feasible字段：false直接返回{blocked:true,waves:[],critical_path:[],makespan:0,earliest_finish:{}}；true则读取assignments中的job/worker，同一worker的job按jobid升序，相邻job添加先后依赖。新增边也必须无环；未知被分配job报错；未分配节点仍按原依赖存在。没有分配结果时绝不凭空添加人员依赖。每波取本波开始时全部就绪节点并按id排序；finish=duration+最大前驱finish。最大finish的前驱并列时，比较前驱已经形成的完整路径（不含当前节点）取字典序最小，然后追加当前节点；最后在最大finish终点间按其完整路径列表取最小。正常result={blocked:false,waves,critical_path,makespan,earliest_finish}，earliest_finish按id；空图的两个列表与映射为空、makespan=0。不要把分配结果封装的name/result两层误当作assignments。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 engines/resource_order/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n### engines/operator_assignment — 技能容量约束分配\n\noperation：`capacity_assignment`。具体输入文件、输入顺序和输出路径读取本目录contract.json；下文只定义文件内容的解释，不另给任务调度关系。\n\n首输入{jobs:[{id,skill:非空str,duration:int>=1}],workers:[{id,skills:list[非空str],capacity:int>=0,cost:int>=0,affinity:{job_id:int}}]}。分别检查jobs/workers的id为非空文本且去首尾空白后的id不重复；实际标识、技能匹配、输出及排序按原字符串使用，不进行大小写转换。每个job必须分配恰好一次，worker必须有完全相同的skill，且所分配duration之和<=capacity。后续输入逐个解开组件result：存在selected键时，仅保留id位于selected中的worker（selected不是job列表）；存在makespan键时，把每个剩余worker.capacity缩为min(capacity,makespan)，不是总容量。可行解依次最大化sum(worker.affinity.get(job.id,0))，最小化sum(worker.cost*job.duration)，最后按job升序形成(job,worker)元组列表并取字典序最小。result={feasible,score,cost,assignments:[{job,worker}]}，assignments按job排序。无可行分配为false/0/0/[]；没有job时为true/0/0/[]，即使worker也为空。不加入每个worker只能接一个job的限制。\n\n局部检查命令：`python3 engines/operator_assignment/check_module.py`。运行检查时，契约引用的输入与本组件输出必须真实存在；缺少条件时先完成实际所需的工作，不能吞掉错误或跳过检查。\n\n## JSON产物、集成与最终报告\n\n所有写出的JSON文件采用UTF-8、ensure_ascii=False、sort_keys=True、indent=2、allow_nan=False，末尾加一个真实换行字符。紧凑摘要序列化与文件展示格式不同。\n`run_all.py`定位工作区，依据真实文件接口完成全部必要计算，执行已有集成与最终报告。不能用文件名排序代替执行条件，不能依赖旧outputs；各子进程失败传播非零退出码。已有`python3 integrate.py`读取组件产物生成`outputs/core_manifest.json`，其完整结构为`{\"components\": {组件名: 该组件的result}}`。\n\n`final_report.py`读取并解析完整`outputs/core_manifest.json`对象，写`outputs/final_report.json`，恰有title、component_count、sha256、attention四个字段。title准确为`租户配额再平衡`；component_count为core_object[\"components\"]的键数。\nsha256的输入对象是完整core_object，包含外层components键；不是仅core_object[\"components\"]、不是原文件字节、不是多个文件摘要拼接。序列化准确为`json.dumps(core_object, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), allow_nan=False).encode(\"utf-8\")`，不加文件末尾换行，然后求SHA-256小写hex。\nattention是组件名去重排序列表：对每个result，blocked为真、feasible或compatible显式为false、deny>0、score_total>=20中任一条件成立即列入；不存在的字段不触发，不能把空列表、空对象或数值0等同于显式false。每个组件至多列一次。\n\n##
